# Community content: quest and script audit (PRIVATE)

Private notes, like `COMMUNITY_MERGE.md`: never in the public tree; say "the community client".

Generated by `python -I tools/community-merge/quest_audit.py --sql server/sql/custom/azeroth_warfare/81_community_fixes.sql` against the local world DB (read-only). Rerun after any re-import.

## Summary

- Imported quests audited: **2371**.
- Completable from data as imported: **929** clean, **181** completable but touching something whose C++ script we lack (giver gossip, target behaviour, item script) - playable, may feel off.
- Blocked as imported: **1261**. Of those, **561** fixed in data by `81_community_fixes.sql` and **700** hidden (an always-false `RequiredCondition`, condition 4190001; no rows deleted). Undo a hide by setting `RequiredCondition` back to 0.
- Working after the fixes: **1671** of 2371 offered to players (1490 clean or fixed, 181 at risk).
- Missing C++ scripts: 225 creatures, 54 objects, 50 items, 6 instance maps.
- Bosses given EventAI: **42** (table below).

| target creature missing/unreachable | 240 |
| quest giver not reachable | 16 |
| event/exploration objective with nothing in data to complete it | 8 |
